      12.</font></div>

                                                    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: times new roman">Security

      <div id="HDR">
        <div id="GLHDR" style="WIDTH: 100%" align="right"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 8pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">&#160;

    <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt"><br>&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="center"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">PART
I</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Item
1.&#160;&#160;Business.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">General</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">American
Eagle Energy Inc. (&#8220;we,&#8221; &#8220;our,&#8221; &#8220;us,&#8221; or the &#8220;Company&#8221;) was incorporated in the
State of Nevada as Yellow Hill Energy Inc. on March 14, 2007 to engage in the
acquisition, exploration, and development of natural resource
properties.&#160;&#160;We are an exploration stage company with no revenues and
a limited operating history.&#160;&#160;Our principal executive office is
located at 27 North 27<font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 70%; VERTICAL-ALIGN: super">th</font> Street,
Suite 21G, Billings, Montana 59101.&#160;&#160;Our telephone number is (406)
294-9765.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Our
independent auditor has issued an audit opinion for the Company, which includes
a statement expressing substantial doubt as to our ability to continue as a
going concern.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">On
December 14, 2010, Jay Jhaveri resigned as President, Secretary, Treasurer, and
a director of our company and Richard Findley was appointed as President,
Secretary, Treasurer, and a director of our company.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">On
January 13, 2010, we issued an aggregate of 10,000,000 restricted shares of our
common stock to four persons in a private transaction.&#160;&#160;The shares
were issued at par value, resulting in total proceeds received of
$10,000.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">On
January 14, 2010, we issued an aggregate of 1,060,000 restricted shares of our
common stock at the price of $0.75 per share, to one non-U.S. person (at that
term as defined in Regulation S of the Securities Act of 1933, as amended (the
&#8220;Securities Act&#8221;)), relying on Regulation S and/or Section 4(2) of the
Securities Act, pursuant to the closing of a private placement, for aggregate
gross proceeds of $795,000.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">On
January 15, 2010, we entered into an Assignment Agreement with Murrayfield
Limited, a United Kingdom company, regarding the acquisition of an interest in
an oil and gas well in Mississippi (the &#8220;Oil Well
Assignment&#8221;).&#160;&#160;Lexaria Corp. and Murrayfield had entered into an
Assignment Agreement, dated October 2, 2009, wherein Murrayfield had acquired
from Lexaria certain interests regarding the PP F-12-4 well (the &#8220;Oil Well&#8221;) in
the Belmont Lake Field in Wilkinson County, Mississippi.&#160;&#160;Murrayfield
assigned its interest in the well to us for $150,000, which consists of the
costs paid by Murrayfield to Lexaria, for the acquisition of its
interest.&#160;&#160;The Oil Well was not drilled as of June 16<font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 70%; VERTICAL-ALIGN: super">th</font>, 2010
due to local flooding conditions and, on that date, we entered into an
Assignment Settlement Agreement with Lexaria to terminate the Oil Well
Assignment.&#160;&#160;In connection therewith, we have been refunded $150,000
less our share of incurred expenses in an attempt to drill the well for a net
amount of approximately $144,000.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">On
January 21, 2010, we also entered into an Assignment Agreement with Murrayfield
Limited, regarding the acquisition of a working interest in an oil and gas lease
in Willacy County, Texas, known as the Sauz Ranch Prospect (the &#8220;Leasehold
Assignment&#8221;).&#160;&#160;Miramar Petroleum Inc. and Murrayfield had entered into
an Assignment of Oil and Gas Lease, dated August 28, 2009, pursuant to a Lease
Purchase and Development Agreement, dated August 5, 2009, wherein Murrayfield
acquired from Miramar certain interests regarding a leasehold interest in
Willacy County, Texas.&#160;&#160;Murrayfield assigned its interest in the lease
to American Eagle for $137,500, which consists of the turnkey costs paid by
Murrayfield to Miramar for the acquisition of its interest.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">With the
Leasehold Assignment, American Eagle acquired a 12.5% working interest in an oil
and gas lease in Willacy County, Texas.&#160;&#160;The lands covered by the
lease consist of 908 acres, out of the San Juan de Carrieitos, Willacy County,
Texas, which covers an undivided 29/32nd of the mineral estate.&#160;&#160;The
area is also subject to another oil and gas lease owned by Exxon Mobil
Corporation for the remaining 3/32nds.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">During
the months from February through July, 2010, we acquired approximately 6,850 net
acres located in Divide County, North Dakota (the &#8220;Spyglass Prospect&#8221;) through
the execution of 171 oil and gas leases.&#160;&#160;On June 18, 2010, we sold
50% of our working interest in the Spyglass Prospect to Eternal Energy Corp.
(&#8220;Eternal&#8221;) and acquired a 50% working interest in approximately 4,320 net acres
located in southeastern Saskatchewan (the &#8220;Hardy Prospect&#8221;) and an existing,
shut-in well bore from Eternal.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div id="PGBRK" style="MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; WIDTH: 100%; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt">
      <div id="FTR">
        <div id="GLFTR" style="WIDTH: 100%" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 8pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">&#160;
</font></div>
      </div>
      <div id="PN" style="PAGE-BREAK-AFTER: always">
        <div style="WIDTH: 100%; TEXT-ALIGN: center"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">1</font></div>
        <div style="WIDTH: 100%; TEXT-ALIGN: center">
          <hr style="COLOR: black" noshade size="2">
        </div>
      </div>
      <div id="HDR">
        <div id="GLHDR" style="WIDTH: 100%" align="right"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 8pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">&#160;
</font></div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">In
addition, we intend to enter into an operating agreement with Eternal, whereby
Eternal will be responsible for managing all future exploration and operational
activities for both the Hardy and Spyglass Prospects.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Bankruptcy
or Similar Proceedings</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">There has
been no bankruptcy, receivership or similar proceeding.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Competition</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman"><font id="TAB1" style="MARGIN-LEFT: 36pt"></font>The oil and gas industry is intensely
competitive.&#160;&#160;We compete with numerous individuals and companies,
including many major oil and gas companies, which have substantially greater
technical, financial, and operational resources and
staffs.&#160;&#160;Accordingly, there is a high degree of competition for
desirable oil and gas leases, suitable properties for drilling operations, and
necessary drilling equipment, as well as for access to funds.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Employees</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">As of
April 30, 2010, we had no employees.&#160;&#160;Our daily activities are
currently managed by Richard Findley, who serves as our President and CEO and
sole Officer and Director.&#160;&#160;Mr. Findley currently devotes
approximately 10 hours per week to company matters.&#160;&#160;He will devote as
much time as the board of directors determines is necessary to manage the
affairs of the company.&#160;&#160;Mr. Findley is not currently compensated for
his services.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">On June
7, 2010, we hired Tom Lantz to serve as our Vice President of
Operations.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Reports
to Securities Holders</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">We
provide an annual report that includes audited financial information to our
stockholders.&#160;&#160;We make our financial information equally available to
any interested parties or investors through compliance with the disclosure rules

    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">An

    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Risks
Related to Our Business and Our Marketplace</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">We
have a limited history operating in the oil and gas industry.&#160;&#160;As a
result, it is difficult to evaluate our business and prospects.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">We have
been in operation for less than two and a half years.&#160;&#160;Thus, we, have
a significantly limited operating and financial history available to help our
stockholders evaluate our past performance.&#160;&#160;Moreover, our limited
historical financial results may not accurately predict our future
performance.&#160;&#160;Companies in their initial stages of development present
substantial business and financial risks and may suffer significant
losses.&#160;&#160;As a result of the risks associated with new companies in
general, it is possible that we may not be successful in implementing our
business strategy.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">If
we acquire additional companies in the future, they could prove difficult to
integrate, disrupt our business, dilute stockholder value, or adversely affect
our operating results.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">We may
decide to make other investments in complementary companies.&#160;&#160;If such
investments are made, it is possible that we may not realize the anticipated
benefits of such investments.&#160;&#160;The success of our acquisition program
will depend on our ability to overcome substantial obstacles, such as the
availability of acquisition candidates, our ability to compete successfully with
other acquirers seeking similar acquisition candidates, the availability of
funds to finance acquisitions, and the availability of management resources to
oversee the operation of acquired businesses.&#160;&#160;Furthermore, we may
have to incur additional debt or issue additional equity securities to fund any
future acquisitions or investments, the issuance of which could be dilutive to
us or our existing stockholders.&#160;&#160;In addition, any profitability may
suffer because of acquisition-related costs or future impairment costs for
acquired goodwill and other intangible assets.&#160;&#160;We have limited
resources, and we can offer no assurance that we will succeed in consummating
any additional acquisitions or that we will be able to integrate and manage any
acquisitions successfully.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">We have
no present commitments, understandings, or plans to acquire other
companies.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">The
&#8220;going concern&#8221; reports by our registered public accounting firm may adversely
affect our business, prospects, financial condition, results of operations, cash
flows and stock price by, among other things, making it more difficult to obtain
debt or equity financing.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Our
independent registered public accounting firm has substantial doubt about our
ability to continue as a going concern and have included an explanatory
paragraph related to our ability to continue as a going concern in their report
for the year-ended April 30, 2010.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div id="PGBRK" style="MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; WIDTH: 100%; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt">
      <div id="FTR">
        <div id="GLFTR" style="WIDTH: 100%" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 8pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">&#160;
</font></div>

        <div style="WIDTH: 100%; TEXT-ALIGN: center"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">3</font></div>

      <div id="HDR">
        <div id="GLHDR" style="WIDTH: 100%" align="right"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 8pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">&#160;
</font></div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Reports
of independent auditors questioning a company&#8217;s ability to continue as a going
concern generally are viewed unfavorably by analysts and
investors.&#160;&#160;This may make it difficult for us to raise additional debt
or equity financing needed for our continued operations or for planned
expansion, particularly if we are unable to attain and maintain profitable
operations in the future.&#160;&#160;Consequently, future losses may adversely
affect our business, prospects, financial condition, results of operations and
cash flows.&#160;&#160;You should review the reports of our independent
registered public accounting firm and its financial statements and unaudited pro
forma financial information before making any investment decision.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">As
our properties are in the exploration stage there can be no assurance that we
will establish commercial discoveries on our properties.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt"><br></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Exploration
for economic reserves of oil and gas is subject to a number of risk
factors.&#160;&#160;Few properties that are explored are ultimately developed
into producing oil and/or gas wells.&#160;&#160;Our properties are in the
exploration stage and, accordingly, have not been proven to contain any
economically viable oil or gas reserves.&#160;&#160;Any profitability in the
future from our business will be dependent upon locating and developing economic
reserves of oil and gas, which itself is subject to numerous risk factors as set
forth herein.&#160;&#160;It is possible that we may not establish commercial
discoveries on any of our properties.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt"><br></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">The
potential profitability of oil and gas ventures depends upon factors beyond our
control.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt"><br></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">The
potential profitability of oil and gas properties is dependent upon many factors
beyond our control.&#160;&#160;For instance, world prices and markets for oil
and gas are unpredictable, highly volatile, potentially subject to governmental
regulation, pegging, controls, or any combination of these and other factors,
and respond to changes in domestic, international, political, social, and
economic environments.&#160;&#160;Additionally, due to worldwide economic
uncertainty, the availability and cost of funds for production and other
expenses have become increasingly difficult, if not impossible, to
project.&#160;&#160;These changes and events will likely materially affect our
financial performance.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt"><br></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Adverse
weather conditions can also hinder drilling operations.&#160;&#160;A productive
well may become uneconomic in the event water or other deleterious substances
are encountered that impair or prevent the production of oil and/or gas from the
well.&#160;&#160;In addition, production from any well may be unmarketable if it
is impregnated with water or other deleterious substances.&#160;&#160;The
marketability of oil and gas which may be acquired or discovered will also be
affected by numerous factors beyond our control.&#160;&#160;These factors
include the proximity and capacity of oil and gas pipelines and processing
equipment, market fluctuations of prices, taxes, royalties, land tenure,
allowable production, and environmental protection.&#160;&#160;These factors
cannot be accurately predicted and the combination of these factors may result
in us not receiving an adequate return on invested capital.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt"><br></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Competition
in the oil and gas industry is highly competitive and there is no assurance that
we will be successful in acquiring properties or leases.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt"><br></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">The oil
and gas industry is intensely competitive.&#160;&#160;We compete with numerous
individuals and companies, including many major oil and gas companies, which
have substantially greater technical, financial, and operational resources and
staffs.&#160;&#160;Accordingly, there is a high degree of competition for
desirable oil and gas leases, suitable properties for drilling operations, and
necessary drilling equipment, as well as for access to funds.&#160;&#160;We
cannot predict if the necessary funds can be raised or that any projected work
will be completed.&#160;&#160;Desirable acreage may not become available or if
it is available for leasing, that we may not be successful in acquiring the
leases.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt"><br></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">The
marketability of natural resources will be affected by numerous factors beyond
our control which may result in us not receiving an adequate return on invested
capital to be profitable or viable.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt"><br></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">The
marketability of natural resources which may be acquired or discovered by us
will be affected by numerous factors beyond our control.&#160;&#160;These
factors include market fluctuations in oil and gas pricing and demand, the
proximity and capacity of natural resource markets and processing equipment,
governmental regulations, land tenure, land use, regulation concerning the
importing and exporting of oil and gas, and environmental protection
regulations.&#160;&#160;The exact effect of these factors cannot be accurately
predicted, but the combination of these factors may result in us not receiving
an adequate return on invested capital to be profitable or
viable.</font></div><br>
    <div id="PGBRK" style="MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; WIDTH: 100%; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt">
      <div id="FTR">
        <div id="GLFTR" style="WIDTH: 100%" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 8pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">&#160;
</font></div>
      </div>
      <div id="PN" style="PAGE-BREAK-AFTER: always">
        <div style="WIDTH: 100%; TEXT-ALIGN: center"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">4</font></div>
        <div style="WIDTH: 100%; TEXT-ALIGN: center">
          <hr style="COLOR: black" noshade size="2">
        </div>
      </div>
      <div id="HDR">
        <div id="GLHDR" style="WIDTH: 100%" align="right"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 8pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">&#160;
</font></div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt"><br>&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Oil
and gas operations are subject to comprehensive regulation, which may cause
substantial delays or require capital outlays in excess of those anticipated,
causing an adverse effect on our operations or financial
performance.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt"><br></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Oil and
gas operations are subject to federal, state, and local laws relating to the
protection of the environment, including laws regulating removal of natural
resources from the ground and the discharge of materials into the
environment.&#160;&#160;Oil and gas operations are also subject to federal,
state, and local laws and regulations which seek to maintain health and safety
standards by regulating the design and use of drilling methods and
equipment.&#160;&#160;Various permits from government bodies are required for
drilling operations to be conducted; no assurance can be given that such permits
will be received.&#160;&#160;Environmental standards imposed by federal,
provincial, or local authorities may be changed and any such changes may have
material adverse effects on our activities.&#160;&#160;Moreover, compliance with
such laws may cause substantial delays or require capital outlays in excess of
those anticipated, thus causing an adverse effect on our
company.&#160;&#160;Additionally, we may be subject to liability for pollution
or other environmental damages that we may elect not to insure against due to
prohibitive premium costs and other reasons.&#160;&#160;To date we have not been
required to spend any material amount on compliance with environmental
regulations.&#160;&#160;However, we may be required to do so in future and this
may affect our ability to expand or maintain our operations.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt"><br></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Exploration
and production activities are subject to certain environmental regulations,
which may prevent or delay the commencement or continuance of our
operations.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt"><br></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">In
general, exploration and production activities are subject to certain federal,
state, and local laws and regulations relating to environmental quality and
pollution control.&#160;&#160;Such laws and regulations increase the costs of
these activities and may prevent or delay the commencement or continuance of a
given operation.&#160;&#160;Compliance with these laws and regulations has not
had a material effect on our operations or financial condition to
date.&#160;&#160;Specifically, we will be subject to legislation regarding
emissions into the environment, water discharges and storage, and disposition of
hazardous wastes.&#160;&#160;In addition, legislation has been enacted that
requires well and facility sites to be abandoned and reclaimed to the
satisfaction of state authorities.&#160;&#160;However, such laws and regulations
are frequently changed and we are unable to predict the ultimate cost of
compliance.&#160;&#160;Generally, environmental requirements do not appear to
affect us any differently, or to any greater or lesser extent, than other
companies operating within the oil and gas industry.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt"><br></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">We
believe that our operations currently comply, in all material respects, with all
applicable environmental regulations.&#160;&#160;We are not insured against all
possible environmental risks.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt"><br></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Exploratory
drilling involves many risks and we may become liable for pollution or other
liabilities, which may have an adverse effect on our financial
position.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt"><br></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Drilling
operations generally involve a high degree of risk.&#160;&#160;Hazards such as
unusual or unexpected geological formations, power outages, labor disruptions,
blow-outs, sour gas leakage, fire, inability to obtain suitable or adequate
machinery, equipment or labor, and other risks are involved.&#160;&#160;We may
become subject to liability for pollution or hazards against which we cannot
adequately insure or that we may elect not to insure.&#160;&#160;Incurring any
such liability may have a material adverse effect on our financial position and
operations.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt"><br></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Any
change to government regulation/administrative practices may have a negative
impact on our ability to operate and our profitability.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt"><br></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">The laws,
regulations, policies, or current administrative practices of any government
body, organization, or regulatory agency in the United States or any other
jurisdiction, may be changed, applied or interpreted in a manner that will
fundamentally alter the ability of our company to carry on our
business.&#160;&#160;The actions, policies or regulations, or changes thereto,
of any government body or regulatory agency, or other special interest groups,
may have a detrimental effect on us.&#160;&#160;Any or all of these situations
may have a negative impact on our ability to operate and/or our
profitably.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt">&#160;</div>

    <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">We
may need additional financing to continue and grow operations, which may not be
available on acceptable terms or at all.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt"><br>&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">We may
need to raise additional funds to finance our operations or to grow our
business.&#160;&#160;Additional financing may not be available on terms or at
times that are favorable to us, or at all.&#160;&#160;If adequate funds are not
available when required or on acceptable terms, we may be unable to continue and
grow our operations.&#160;&#160;In addition, such financing transactions, if
successful, may result in additional dilution of our
stockholders.&#160;&#160;Additional financings may also result in the issuance
of securities with rights, preferences, and other characteristics superior to
those of the common stock and, in the case of debt or preferred stock
financings, may subject us to covenants that restrict our ability to operate our
business freely.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-STYLE: italic">We may not
successfully grow our business nor manage our growth, if
any</font>.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Our
success is significantly dependent on a successful acquisition, drilling,
completion, and production program. We may be unable to locate recoverable
reserves or operate on a profitable basis, which would place a significant
strain on our management and administrative, operational, and financial
resources. If our business plan is not successful, and we are not able to
operate profitably, investors may lose some or all of their investment in our
company. In addition, if we are unable to manage our expected growth
effectively, our business could be harmed. There is no assurance that we will be
able to expand our operations or effectively manage any growth.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Risks
Associated with Investing in our Common Stock</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Our
securities are considered highly speculative.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Our
securities must be considered highly speculative, generally because of the
nature of our business and the early stage of our exploration and development
operations.&#160;&#160;We have neither generated any material revenues nor have
we realized a profit from our operations to date and there is little likelihood
that we will generate any revenues or realize any profits in the short
term.&#160;&#160;Since we have not generated any material revenues, we expect
that we will need to raise additional funds through the sale of our equity
securities or debt in order to effectuate our business plan and continue our
business operations.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt"><br></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">We
are not required to meet or maintain any listing standards for our common stock
to be quoted on the OTC Bulletin Board or in the Pink OTC Markets, which could
affect our stockholders&#8217; ability to access trading information about our common
stock.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">The OTC
Bulletin Board and the Pink OTC Markets are each separate and distinct from The
Nasdaq Stock Market, Inc., and any national stock exchange, such as the New York
Stock Exchange or the NYSE Amex.&#160;&#160;Although the OTC Bulletin Board is a
regulated quotation service operated by the Financial Industry Regulatory
Authority, Inc. (&#8220;FINRA&#8221;) that displays real-time quotes, last sale prices, and
volume information in over-the-counter (&#8220;OTC&#8221;) equity securities like our common
stock, and although Pink OTC Markets&#8217; Electronic Quotation Service is an
Internet-based, real-time quotation service for OTC equities for market-makers
and brokers that provides pricing and financial information for the OTC
securities markets, we are not required to meet or maintain any qualitative or
quantitative standards for our common stock to be quoted on either the OTC
Bulletin Board or in the Pink OTC Markets.&#160;&#160;Our common stock does not
presently meet the minimum listing standards for listing on the Nasdaq Stock
Market or any national securities exchange, which could affect our stockholders&#8217;
ability to access trading information about our common stock.&#160;&#160;We are
required, however, to satisfy the reporting requirements under the Exchange
Act.&#160;&#160;If we fail to do so, our shares may no longer be quoted on the
OTC Bulletin Board.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div id="PGBRK" style="MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt">
      <div id="FTR">
        <div id="GLFTR" style="WIDTH: 100%" align="left">
        </div>
      </div>
      <div id="PN" style="PAGE-BREAK-AFTER: always">
        <div style="WIDTH: 100%; TEXT-ALIGN: center"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">6</font></div>
        <div style="WIDTH: 100%; TEXT-ALIGN: center">
          <hr style="COLOR: black" noshade size="2">
        </div>
      </div>
      <div id="HDR">
        <div id="GLHDR" style="WIDTH: 100%" align="right">
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Trading
of our common stock may be restricted by the SEC&#8217;s &#8220;Penny Stock&#8221; regulations
which may limit a stockholder&#8217;s ability to buy and sell our
stock.</font></div>&#160;<br>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">The &#8220;SEC
has adopted regulations that generally define a &#8220;penny stock&#8221; to be any equity
security that has a market price (as defined) less than $5.00 per share or an
exercise price of less than $5.00 per share, subject to certain
exceptions.&#160;&#160;Our securities are covered by the penny stock rules,
which impose additional sales practice requirements on broker-dealers who sell
to persons other than established customers and &#8220;accredited
investors.&#8221;&#160;&#160;The term &#8220;accredited investor&#8221; refers generally to
institutions with assets in excess of $5,000,000 or individuals with a net worth
in excess of $1,000,000 or annual income exceeding $200,000 or $300,000 jointly
with their spouse.&#160;&#160;The penny stock rules require a broker-dealer,
prior to a transaction in a penny stock not otherwise exempt from the rules, to
deliver a standardized risk disclosure document in a form prepared by the SEC
which provides information about penny stocks and the nature and level of risks
in the penny stock market.&#160;&#160;The broker-dealer also must provide the
customer with current bid and offer quotations for the penny stock, the
compensation of the broker-dealer and its salesperson in the transaction and
monthly account statements showing the market value of each penny stock held in
the customer&#8217;s account.&#160;&#160;The bid and offer quotations, and the
broker-dealer and salesperson compensation information, must be given to the
customer orally or in writing prior to effecting the transaction and must be
given to the customer in writing before or with the customer&#8217;s
confirmation.&#160;&#160;In addition, the penny stock rules require that, prior
to a transaction in a penny stock not otherwise exempt from these rules, the
broker-dealer must make a special written determination that the penny stock is
a suitable investment for the purchaser and receive the purchaser&#8217;s written
agreement to the transaction.&#160;&#160;These disclosure requirements may have
the effect of reducing the level of trading activity in the secondary market for
the stock that is subject to these penny stock rules.&#160;&#160;Consequently,
these penny stock rules may affect the ability of broker-dealers to trade our
securities.&#160;&#160;We believe that the penny stock rules discourage investor
interest in and limit the marketability of, our common stock.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Stockholders
should be aware that, according to the SEC, the market for penny stocks has
suffered in recent years from patterns of fraud and abuse.&#160;&#160;Such
patterns include (i) control of the market for the security by one or a few
broker-dealers that are often related to the promoter or issuer; (ii)
manipulation of prices through prearranged matching of purchases and sales and
false and misleading press releases; (iii) &#8220;boiler room&#8221; practices involving
high-pressure sales tactics and unrealistic price projections by inexperienced
sales persons; (iv) excessive and undisclosed bid-ask differentials and markups
by selling broker-dealers; and (v) the wholesale dumping of the same securities
by promoters and broker-dealers after prices have been manipulated to a desired
consequent investor losses.&#160;&#160;Our management is aware of the abuses
that have occurred historically in the penny stock market.&#160;&#160;Although
we do not expect to be in a position to dictate the behavior of the market or of
broker-dealers who participate in the market, management will strive within the
confines of practical limitations to prevent the described patterns from being
established with respect to our common stock.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">FINRA
sales practice requirements may also limit a stockholder&#8217;s ability to buy and
sell our stock.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">In
addition to the &#8220;penny stock&#8221; rules described above, FINRA has adopted rules
that require that in recommending an investment to a customer, a broker-dealer
must have reasonable grounds for believing that the investment is suitable for
that customer.&#160;&#160;Prior to recommending speculative low priced
securities to their non-institutional customers, broker-dealers must make
reasonable efforts to obtain information about the customer&#8217;s financial status,
tax status, investment objectives, and other information.&#160;&#160;Under
interpretations of these rules, FINRA believes that there is a high probability
that speculative low priced securities will not be suitable for at least some
customers.&#160;&#160;FINRA&#8217;s requirements make it more difficult for
broker-dealers to recommend that their customers buy our common stock, which may
limit your ability to buy and sell our stock and have an adverse effect on the
market for our shares.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Our
common stock has been thinly traded and, as a result, you may be unable to sell
at or near ask prices or at all if you need to liquidate your
shares.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">The
shares of our common stock have been, and may continue to be, thinly-traded on
the OTC Bulletin Board, meaning that the number of persons interested in
purchasing shares at or near ask prices at any given time may be relatively
small or non-existent.&#160;&#160;This situation is attributable to a number of
factors, including the fact that we are a small company that is relatively
unknown to stock analysts, stock brokers, institutional investors, and others in
the investment community that generate or influence sales volume and, that even
if we came to the attention of such persons, they tend to be risk-averse and
would be reluctant to follow an unproven company such as ours or purchase or
recommend the purchase of our shares of common stock until such time as we
become more seasoned and viable.&#160;&#160;As a consequence, there may be
periods of several days or more when trading activity in our shares of common
stock is minimal or non-existent, as compared to a seasoned issuer that has a
large and steady volume of trading activity that will generally support
continuous sales without an adverse effect on securities price.&#160;&#160;We
cannot give you any assurance that a broader or more active public trading
market for our shares of common stock will develop or be sustained or that any
trading levels will be sustained.&#160;&#160;Due to these conditions, we cannot
provide any assurances that our stockholders will be able to sell their shares
at or near ask prices or at all.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">In the
past, following periods of volatility in the market price of a company&#8217;s
securities, securities class-action litigation has often been
instituted.&#160;&#160;Such litigation, if instituted, could result in
substantial costs for us and a diversion of management&#8217;s attention and
resources.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div id="PGBRK" style="MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; WIDTH: 100%; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt">
      <div id="FTR">
        <div id="GLFTR" style="WIDTH: 100%" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 8pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">&#160;
</font></div>
      </div>
      <div id="PN" style="PAGE-BREAK-AFTER: always">
        <div style="WIDTH: 100%; TEXT-ALIGN: center"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">7</font></div>
        <div style="WIDTH: 100%; TEXT-ALIGN: center">
          <hr style="COLOR: black" noshade size="2">
        </div>
      </div>
      <div id="HDR">
        <div id="GLHDR" style="WIDTH: 100%" align="right"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 8pt; COLOR: #000000; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">&#160;
</font></div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">The
price of our common stock could be highly volatile.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">It is
likely that our common stock will be subject to price volatility, low volumes of
trades, and large spreads in bid and ask prices quoted by market
makers.&#160;&#160;Due to the low volume of shares that may be traded on any
trading day, persons buying or selling in relatively small quantities may easily
influence prices of our common stock.&#160;&#160;This low volume of trades could
also cause the price of our stock to fluctuate greatly, with large percentage
changes in price occurring in any trading day session.&#160;&#160;Holders of our
common stock may also not be able to liquidate their investment readily or may
be forced to sell at depressed prices due to low volume trading.&#160;&#160;If
high spreads between the bid and ask prices of our common stock exist at the
time of a purchase, the price of the common stock would need to appreciate
substantially on a relative percentage basis for an investor to recoup an
investment in our shares.&#160;&#160;Broad market fluctuations and general
economic and political conditions may also adversely affect the market price of
our common stock.&#160;&#160;No assurance can be given that an orderly and
active market in our common stock will develop or be sustained.&#160;&#160;If an
orderly and active market does not develop, holders of our common stock may be
unable to sell their shares, if at all.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Investors&#8217;
interests in us will be diluted and investors may suffer dilution in net book
value per share if we issue additional shares or raise funds through the sale of
equity securities.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Current
stockholders will be immediately and substantially diluted upon a merger or a
business combination.&#160;&#160;Also, in the event that we are required to
issue any additional shares or enter into private placements to raise financing
through the sale of equity securities, investors&#8217; interests in us will be
diluted and investors may suffer dilution in their net book value per share
depending on the price at which such securities are sold.&#160;&#160;If we issue
any such additional shares, such issuances also will cause a reduction in the
proportionate ownership and voting power of all other
stockholders.&#160;&#160;Further, any such issuance may result in a change in
our control.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-STYLE: italic; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">We
do not anticipate paying cash dividends on our common stock.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">We have
never declared or paid any cash dividends on our common stock and do not
anticipate paying any cash dividends on our common stock in the foreseeable
future.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Item
2.&#160;&#160;Properties.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">We
currently utilize office space at 27 North 27<font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 70%; VERTICAL-ALIGN: super">th</font> Street,
Suite 21G, Billings, Montana 59101.&#160;&#160;We do not currently own any
physical assets.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Item
3.&#160;&#160;Legal Proceedings.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">We are
not currently involved in any legal proceedings and we are not aware of any
pending or potential legal actions.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Item
4.&#160;&#160;Submission of Matters to a Vote of Security Holders.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">No matter
was submitted to a vote of security holders, through the solicitation of proxies
or otherwise, during the year ended April 30, 2010.</font></div>

    <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt"><br></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">On August
11, 2010, we had approximately 12 stockholders of record.&#160;&#160;We have
never declared or paid any cash dividends on our common stock.&#160;&#160;For
the foreseeable future, we expect to retain any earnings to finance the
operation and expansion of our business.</font></div>

    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">The

    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Item
7.&#160;&#160;Management&#8217;s Discussion and Analysis of Financial Condition and
Results of Operations.</font></div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
    <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">
      <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">THE
FOLLOWING PRESENTATION OF OUR MANAGEMENT&#8217;S DISCUSSION AND ANALYSIS SHOULD BE
READ IN CONJUNCTION WITH THE FINANCIAL STATEMENTS AND OTHER FINANCIAL
INFORMATION INCLUDED ELSEWHERE IN THIS REPORT.</font></div>
      <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
      <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">The
following discussion of the financial condition and results of operation of the
Company should be read in conjunction with the financial statements and the
notes to those statements included herein.&#160;&#160;This discussion includes
forward-looking statements that involve risk and
uncertainties.&#160;&#160;Actual results may differ materially from those
anticipated in the forward-looking statements.</font></div>
      <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
      <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Industry
Overview</font></div>
      <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="justify">&#160;</div>
      <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 18pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman"><font id="TAB1" style="MARGIN-LEFT: 18pt"></font>The petroleum industry is highly
competitive and subject to significant volatility due to numerous market
forces.&#160;&#160;Crude oil and natural gas prices are affected by market
fundamentals such as weather, inventory levels, competing fuel prices, overall
demand, and the availability of supply.</font></div>
      <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt"><br></div>
      <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 18pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman"><font id="TAB1" style="MARGIN-LEFT: 18pt"></font>Worldwide oil prices reached historical
highs during the last half of 2008, before tumbling amid worldwide economic
crisis.&#160;&#160;Oil prices stabilized during 2009 and remained stable through
the early part of 2010.&#160;&#160;Future economic instability could impact
demand, caused by a consumer shift to alternative fuel sources and/or supply,
driven largely by concerns regarding the economic viability of extracting
natural resources, thus affecting crude oil prices.</font></div>
      <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt"><br></div>
      <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 18pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman"><font id="TAB1" style="MARGIN-LEFT: 18pt"></font>Oil prices have significantly affected
profitability and returns for upstream producers.&#160;&#160;Oil prices cannot
be predicted with any certainty.&#160;&#160;During the past ten years the
industry has experienced wide fluctuations in prices.&#160;&#160;While local
supply/demand fundamentals are a decisive factor affecting domestic natural gas
prices over the long term, day-to-day prices may be more volatile in the futures
markets, such as on the NYMEX and other exchanges, making it difficult to
forecast prices with any degree of confidence.</font></div>
      <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt"><br></div>
      <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-WEIGHT: bold; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Company
Overview</font></div>
      <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt"><br></div>
      <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">The
address of our principal executive office is 27 North 27<font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 70%; VERTICAL-ALIGN: super">th</font> Street,
Suite 21G, Billings, MT, 59101.&#160;&#160;Our telephone number is
406-294-9765.</font></div>
      <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt"><br></div>
      <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Our
common stock is quoted on the OTC Bulletin Board under the symbol
&#8220;AMZG&#8221;.</font></div>
      <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt"><br></div>
      <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">Our
Company was incorporated in the State of Nevada under the name &#8220;Yellow Hill
Energy Inc.&#8221; on March 14, 2007 and is engaged in the acquisition, exploration
and development of natural resource properties of merit.&#160;&#160;On October
5, 2009, we filed documents with the Nevada Secretary of State to affect a
change of our name from &#8220;Yellow Hill Energy Inc.&#8221; to &#8220;American Eagle Energy
Inc.&#8221; by way of a merger with our wholly owned subsidiary, American Eagle Energy
Inc., which was formed solely to facilitate the name change.</font></div>
      <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt"><br></div>
      <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">On June
18, 2010, we formed a wholly owned subsidiary named AEE Canada Inc. for the
purpose of conducting operations and holding title to certain assets located
within Canada.</font></div>
      <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt"><br></div>
      <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">We have
not been involved in any bankruptcy, receivership or similar proceedings, nor
have we been a party to any material reclassifications, merger, consolidation or
purchase or sale of a significant amount of assets not in the ordinary course of
our business.</font></div>
      <div style="DISPLAY: block; TEXT-INDENT: 0pt"><br></div>
      <div style="DISPLAY: block; MARGIN-LEFT: 0pt; TEXT-INDENT: 36pt; MARGIN-RIGHT: 0pt" align="left"><font style="DISPLAY: inline; FONT-SIZE: 10pt; FONT-FAMILY: Times New Roman">On
October 9, 2008, Scott Lindsay resigned as our President, Chief Financial
Officer and Director and Sean Mitchell resigned as our Secretary, Treasurer and
Director.&#160;&#160;As a result, on October 9, 2008, Jay Jhaveri was appointed
by us to serve in these roles.&#160;&#160;On December 14, 2009, Mr. Jhaveri
resigned and was replaced by Richard Findley.&#160;&#160;Mr. Findley currently
serves as our President, Chief Financial Officer, Secretary, Treasurer and
Director.&#160;&#160;In performing his duties, Mr. Findley currently devotes
approximately 10 hours per week, for which he receives no monetary or
stock-based compensation.</font></div><br>
